Technology reports:
+ alien vessel hull [alnhul]: Recovered alien hull geometry. Automated internals need no crew and no
  life support.
  - alien vessel hull [alnhul]: Crashed alien hull. Automated internals need no crew and no life
    support.
+ advanced robo-factory [robofc]: An unmanned alien fabrication plant. It has no crew requirement.
  - advanced robo-factory [robofc]: Unmanned alien fabrication plant. No crew and no life support.
+ automated command module [autcmd]: A crewless command core that can wake nested alien systems.
  - automated command module [autcmd]: Crewless alien command core.
+ automated propulsion module [autdrv]: A crewless reaction drive sized for an alien hull.
  - automated propulsion module [autdrv]: Crewless alien drive. No life support.
+ automated helium powerplant [he3aut]: An unmanned helium-3 fusion plant with no crew stations.
  - automated helium powerplant [he3aut]: Unmanned helium-3 fusion plant. No crew.
+ small scale transportation [strans]: Transport over interstellar distances and storage of freight
  poses logistics problems.
  - small cargo bay [cargob]: The cargo bays may hold a wide variety of cargo for bulk
    transportations.
+ fighter drone bay [drnbay]: A hangar that stores and launches a squad of fighter drones.
  - fighter drone bay [drnbay]: Hangar for a squad of crewless fighter drones.
+ alien fighter drone [alndrn]: A crewless alien fighter. It needs no life support.
  - alien fighter drone [alndrn]: Crewless alien fighter. No life support.
